# Tera Shell Necklace

[Tera Shell Necklace]
Type: Necklace ([[Necklaces]])
Rarity: EPIC ([[Rarity]])

[Item Properties]
Enchantable: ✔ Yes
Reforgeable: ✔ Yes

The Tera Shell Necklace is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Necklaces|Necklace]] that disables the ability to catch [[Trophy (disambiguation)|Trophies]] while equipped.
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|4x [[Enchanted Mycelium Cube]] 4x [[Enchanted Red Sand Cube]] 5x [[Gazing Pearl]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Red Sand Cube]] ×4 + [[Enchanted Mycelium Cube]] ×4 + [[Gazing Pearl]] x5 → Tera Shell Necklace|
## Usage
Necklace:
Tera Shell Necklace [[Health]]: +80 [[Defense]]: +30 [[Intelligence]]: +20
[Effects]
[You can no longer catch Trophy Fish while wearing this Necklace.]

## Notes
- The Tera Shell Necklace does not prevent obtaining [[Trophy (disambiguation)|Trophies]] that are obtained from other sources than direct catching, such as [[Trophy Fish|Golden Fish]], [[Trophy Fish|Obfuscated-1]], [[Trophy Frogs|Exploding Frog]] or [[Puddle Jumper]].
## Trivia
- Tera Shell Necklace nullifies the effects of [[Trophy Chance]], without actually affecting the stat.

The Terra Shard is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Earth Elemental]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Fusion
A Terra Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Special Fusion]]. For [[Attribute Fusion|Special Fusion]], the following input conditions must be met:
- First: [[Rarity|EPIC]] and Forest Category
- Second: [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] or higher
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Terra Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Earth Elemental]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Earth Elemental ([[Attributes]]); Grants +2–20 [[Health]].
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].

The Thief Skin is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Helmet Skins|Helmet Skin]] that could be purchased from a [[Fire Sale]].
## Obtaining
The Thief Skin could be purchased from the [[Fire Sale]] hosted on February 3, 2024. Being [[Rarity|EPIC]], it cost 650 [[SkyBlock Gems]] and had a limited stock of 1,000 purchases.
## Usage
The Thief Skin can be applied to a [[Goblin Armor|Goblin Helmet]] by combining it in an [[Anvil]] to change its appearance.

[Total]
[Thunder Armor Health: +890 Defense: +780 Strength: +100 Sea Creature Chance: +17.5]
[Effects]
[Tiered Bonus: Static Charge; Gains 1 static charge every 30 (2 Pieces)➜25 (3 Pieces)➜20 (4 Pieces)➜10 (5 Pieces) seconds.; Hitting a target will cause a discharge, adding 15%➜20%➜25%➜40% damage to that hit for each charge collected.; Maximum Charge Capacity: 2➜3➜4➜5; Pieces Required: 2]
Upon having at least 2 pieces equipped, you unlock the Static Charge Tiered Bonus, which displays Static Charges as lightning bolts in your action bar. You gain 1 charge periodically, with the cooldown between charges lowering for each piece equipped. Hitting an enemy with a melee consumes all charges, causing lightning to hit your target and increase the damage of that attack, with more damage the more Static Charges you have stored. This does not trigger with magic or ranged damage.

# Thunderling
Categories: Epic items, Items in Sacks, Items, Mutations
[Thunderling]
Type: Mutation
Rarity: EPIC ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✕ No
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Sacks: Small Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]]); Medium Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]]); Large Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]])
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: THUNDERLING
Tooltip: This plant hums with electricity growing more volatile the longer it's left unchecked. If you don't release its charge in time, it'll shock anything nearby, yourself included.; Analyze this Mutation at the Crop Analyzer in Jake's Underground Lab.; EPIC MUTATION
A Thunderling is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Mutations|Mutation]].
## Obtaining
A Thunderling can grow in the [[Greenhouse]] if there are 5 [[Soggybud]] and 3 [[Noctilume]] within 1 block of farmland.
While growing, a Thunderling does not need water and has 16 Growth Stages. When placed near another crop, it spreads buffs and debuffs to adjacent crops.
A Thunderling gains charge as it grows, filling up a progress bar. If a Thunderling has more than 16000 charge, it will stop growing until discharged. The player must right-click it to get rid of charge. Additionally, discharging while holding a [[Empty Thunder Bottle|Thunder in a Bottle]], [[Empty Storm Bottle|Storm in a Bottle]], or [[Empty Hurricane Bottle|Hurricane in a Bottle]] provides charge, equivalent to the current charge shown in the Growth Status (2000 for every growth stage).
When harvesting without any of the bottles in hand, the discharge deals 30% of the player's current [[Health]] as [[True Damage]] to the player.
## Crop Drops
When harvested, Thunderlings also drop 1,650 (25 stacks plus 50) [[Cactus]], 4,400 (68 stacks plus 48) [[Melon Slice]], 4,400 [[Wild Rose]], and +17,600 [[Farming|Farming XP]].
## Usage
### Crop Analyzer
A Thunderling can be analyzed in the [[Crop Analyzer]] for 800,000 [[Coins]]. When analyzed, it grants 400 [[Copper]]. The first time analyzing a Thunderling grants an additional 1,000 [[Copper]].
### Mutations
Thunderling are used to create a [[PlantBoy Advance]].
## Game Message
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|Discharging with hand|OUCH! The Thunderling has discharged all his energy, frying you!|
|Discharging with [[Empty Thunder Bottle]],; [[Empty Storm Bottle]],; or [[Empty Hurricane Bottle]]|ZOOP! Your [item name] has absorbed [amount] charge, calming your Thunderling.|
## Trivia
- Despite dealing 30% of the player's [[Health]] as [[True Damage]], a Thunderling's discharge can never lead to the player's death, as it deals [[True Damage]] based on their current [[Health]] instead of their maximum.
## Gallery
- Thunderling in the Greenhouse Thunderling in the [[Greenhouse]]
## History

|Sep 30, 2026|Going over max charge no longer destroys the Mutation.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]

|Oct 6, 2026|0.27.2|Going over max charge no longer destroys the Mutation.|

The Titanium Artifact is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Accessories|Accessory]] that grants +45 [[Mining Speed]]. It can be obtained from forging in [[The Forge]] in the [[Dwarven Mines]].
## Obtaining
The Titanium Artifact can be forged once the player has reached [[Heart of the Mountain|Heart of the Mountain IV (4)]]. It requires 12 [[Refined Titanium]], 1 [[Titanium Ring]], and 1 day and 12 hours to forge.
### Forging Tree
- [[Titanium Artifact]] 1 Titanium Artifact
  - 12 [[Refined Titanium]] 192 (3 stacks) [[Enchanted Titanium]] 30,720 (480 stacks) [[Titanium]]
  - 1 [[Titanium Ring]] 6 [[Refined Titanium]] 96 (1 stack plus 32) [[Enchanted Titanium]] 15,360 (240 stacks) [[Titanium]] 1 [[Titanium Talisman]] 2 [[Refined Titanium]] 32 [[Enchanted Titanium]] 5,120 (80 stacks) [[Titanium]]
## Upgrading
The Titanium Artifact can be upgraded into the [[Titanium Relic]] by casting 20 [[Refined Titanium]] with a Titanium Artifact in [[The Forge]]. It requires [[Heart of the Mountain]] Tier 5 and takes 3 days to upgrade.
## Trivia
- Upgrading this accessory will remove all [[Enrichments]] and [[Recombobulator 3000]] applied when it is used in the Forge to obtain the next version of the accessory.
- The mining speed of this talisman used to stack with its predecessor variants ([[Titanium Relic]], [[Titanium Ring]] and [[Titanium Talisman]]) and gave a total of 150 [[Mining Speed]].
  - This has now been patched.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jan 15, 2021|0.11|Added Titanium Artifact.|
|Unknown Date|Buffed the Titanium Artifact's [[Mining Speed]] from 30 to 45.|
|Oct 5, 2021|Fixed Titanium Artifact, Titanium Ring, Titanium Talisman and Titanium Relic Stacking.|

The Titanium Drill DR-X355 is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Drills|Drill]].
## Obtaining
The Titanium Drill DR-X355 can be forged once the player has reached [[Heart of the Mountain|Heart of the Mountain V (5)]]. It takes 4 hours to forge.
### Forging Tree
- [[Titanium Drill DR-X355]] 1 Titanium Drill DR-X355
  - 8 [[Refined Titanium]] 128 (2 stacks) [[Enchanted Titanium]] 20,480 (320 stacks) [[Titanium]]
  - 8 [[Refined Mithril]] 1,280 (20 stacks) [[Enchanted Mithril]] 204,800 (3,200 stacks) [[Mithril]]
  - 1 [[Drill Motor]] 10 [[Treasurite]] 1 [[Enchanted Iron Block]] 160 (2 stacks plus 32) [[Enchanted Iron Ingot]] 25,600 (400 stacks) [[Iron Ingot]] 3 [[Enchanted Redstone Block]] 480 (7 stacks plus 32) [[Enchanted Redstone Dust]] 76,800 (1,200 stacks) [[Redstone Dust]] 1 [[Golden Plate]] 1 [[Refined Diamond]] 2 [[Enchanted Diamond Block]] 320 (5 stacks) [[Enchanted Diamond]] 51,200 (800 stacks) [[Diamond]] 2 [[Enchanted Gold Block]] 320 [[Enchanted Gold Ingot]] 51,200 [[Gold Ingot]] 5 [[Glacite Jewel]]
  - 1 [[Fuel Canister]] 2 [[Enchanted Coal Block]] 320 [[Enchanted Coal]] 51,200 [[Coal]]
  - 6 [[Golden Plate]] 6 [[Refined Diamond]] 12 [[Enchanted Diamond Block]] 1,920 (30 stacks) [[Enchanted Diamond]] 307,200 (4,800 stacks) [[Diamond]] 12 [[Enchanted Gold Block]] 1,920 [[Enchanted Gold Ingot]] 307,200 [[Gold Ingot]] 30 [[Glacite Jewel]]
## Usage
Drill:
Titanium Drill DR-X355 [[Damage]]: +75 [[Breaking Power]]: +7 [[Mining Speed]]: +700 [[Mining Fortune]]: +25
The Titanium Drill DR-X355 has a base 3,000 [[Machine Fuel|Fuel]], which can be increased through a [[Drills|Fuel Tank]]. Additionally, a [[Drills|Drill Engine]] and [[Drills|Upgrade Module]] can be added to the drill through a [[Drill Anvil]] to grant further bonuses.
When breaking blocks, the Titanium Drill DR-X355's [[Machine Fuel|Fuel]] will deplete. Most resources will remove 1 [[Machine Fuel|Fuel]] when mined; [[Gemstone|Aquamarine]], [[Gemstone|Citrine]], [[Gemstone|Onyx]] and [[Gemstone|Peridot]] remove 2 [[Machine Fuel|Fuel]] instead; [[Glacite]], [[Umber]] and [[Tungsten]] remove 3 [[Machine Fuel|Fuel]] instead. [[Machine Fuel|Fuel]] can be regained by adding Fuel to the Drill through a [[Drill Anvil]].
### Forging
The Titanium Drill DR-X355, along with a variety of other items, can be used to forge a [[Titanium Drill DR-X455]] once the player has reached [[Heart of the Mountain|Heart of the Mountain V (5)]]. It takes 30 seconds to forge.
#### Forging Tree
- 1 [[Titanium Drill DR-X455]]

  - 10 [[Refined Diamond]] 20 [[Enchanted Diamond Block]] 3,200 (50 stacks) [[Enchanted Diamond]] 512,000 (8,000 stacks) [[Diamond]]
  - 12 [[Refined Titanium]] 192 (3 stacks) [[Enchanted Titanium]] 30,720 (480 stacks) [[Titanium]]
  - 5 [[Mithril Plate]] 5 [[Refined Titanium]] 80 (1 stack plus 16) [[Enchanted Titanium]] 12,800 (200 stacks) [[Titanium]] 25 [[Refined Mithril]] 4,000 (62 stacks plus 32) [[Enchanted Mithril]] 640,000 (10,000 stacks) [[Mithril]] 5 [[Golden Plate]] 5 [[Refined Diamond]] 10 [[Enchanted Diamond Block]] 1,600 (25 stacks) [[Enchanted Diamond]] 256,000 (4,000 stacks) [[Diamond]] 10 [[Enchanted Gold Block]] 1,600 [[Enchanted Gold Ingot]] 256,000 [[Gold Ingot]] 25 [[Glacite Jewel]] 5 [[Enchanted Iron Block]] 800 (12 stacks plus 32) [[Enchanted Iron Ingot]] 128,000 (2,000 stacks) [[Iron Ingot]]
## Trivia
- It takes a total of 390 hours (16 days and 6 hours) to forge the materials and the drill. It can be decreased with the [[Heart of the Mountain|Quick Forge]] perk and multiple [[The Forge|Forge]] slots to 54.6 hours (2 days and 6.6 hours).

## Usage
Drill:
Titanium Drill DR-X555 [[Damage]]: +75 [[Breaking Power]]: +9 [[Mining Speed]]: +1,200 [[Mining Fortune]]: +70
The Titanium Drill DR-X555 has a base 3,000 [[Machine Fuel|Fuel]], which can be increased through a [[Drills|Fuel Tank]]. Additionally, a [[Drills|Drill Engine]] and [[Drills|Upgrade Module]] can be added to the drill through a [[Drill Anvil]] to grant further bonuses.
When breaking blocks, the Titanium Drill DR-X555's [[Machine Fuel|Fuel]] will deplete. Most resources will remove 1 [[Machine Fuel|Fuel]] when mined; [[Gemstone|Aquamarine]], [[Gemstone|Citrine]], [[Gemstone|Onyx]] and [[Gemstone|Peridot]] remove 2 [[Machine Fuel|Fuel]] instead; [[Glacite]], [[Umber]] and [[Tungsten]] remove 3 [[Machine Fuel|Fuel]] instead. [[Machine Fuel|Fuel]] can be regained by adding Fuel to the Drill through a [[Drill Anvil]].
### Forging
The Titanium Drill DR-X555, along with a variety of other items, can be used to forge a [[Titanium Drill DR-X655]] once the player has reached [[Heart of the Mountain|Heart of the Mountain VI (6)]]. It takes 30 seconds to forge.

- It takes a total of 2760 hours (115 days) to forge the materials and the drill. It can be decreased with the [[Heart of the Mountain|Quick Forge]] perk and multiple [[The Forge|Forge]] slots to 386.4 hours (16 days and 2.4 hours).
  - The upgrade from [[Titanium Drill DR-X455]] takes 1654 hours (68 days and 22 hours) to forge, or 231.56 hours (9 days and 15.56 hours) if included the factors above.[Confirm (Confirmation needed; Is it accounting for 0.20.6?; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)]

## Usage
Drill:
Topaz Drill KGR-12 [[Damage]]: +75 [[Breaking Power]]: +9 [[Mining Speed]]: +450 [[Pristine]]: +1.5 [[Gemstone Fortune]]: +100
[Effects]
[Grants +15% ᠅ Gemstone Powder, and +800 Mining Speed when mining Gemstones.]
The Topaz Drill KGR-12 has a base 3,000 [[Machine Fuel|Fuel]], which can be increased through a [[Drills|Fuel Tank]]. Additionally, a [[Drills|Drill Engine]] and [[Drills|Upgrade Module]] can be added to the drill through a [[Drill Anvil]] to grant further bonuses.
When breaking blocks, the Topaz Drill KGR-12's [[Machine Fuel|Fuel]] will deplete. Most resources will remove 1 [[Machine Fuel|Fuel]] when mined; [[Gemstone|Aquamarine]], [[Gemstone|Citrine]], [[Gemstone|Onyx]] and [[Gemstone|Peridot]] remove 2 [[Machine Fuel|Fuel]] instead; [[Glacite]], [[Umber]] and [[Tungsten]] remove 3 [[Machine Fuel|Fuel]] instead. [[Machine Fuel|Fuel]] can be regained by adding Fuel to the Drill through a [[Drill Anvil]].
This drill also gives a 15% Extra ᠅ [[Gemstone Powder]] which makes it good for powder mining.
### Forging
The Topaz Drill KGR-12, along with a variety of other items, can be used to forge a [[Jasper Drill X]] once the player has reached [[Heart of the Mountain|Heart of the Mountain VI (6)]]. It takes 30 seconds to forge.

The Toucan Parrot Skin is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Pet Skins|Pet Skin]] that could be obtained from a past [[Taylor|Seasonal Bundle]] from [[Taylor]].
## Obtaining
The Toucan Parrot Skin could be purchased from the Spring 2024 [[Taylor|Seasonal Bundle]] from [[Taylor]]. Being [[Rarity|EPIC]], it cost 650 [[SkyBlock Gems]]. However, due to being before the Winter 2024 Bundle, it cannot appear in future bundles, making it now unobtainable.
## Usage
The Toucan Parrot Skin can be applied to the player's summoned [[Parrot Pet]] by right-clicking it to change its particle trail and appearance.

The Toucan Shard is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Why Not More]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Fusion
A Toucan Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Special Fusion]]. For [[Attribute Fusion|Special Fusion]], the following input conditions must be met:
- First: Bird Family
- Second: [[Rarity|EPIC]] or higher
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Toucan Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Why Not More]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Why Not More ([[Attributes]]); Your pet's EXP Share grants +1%–10% more EXP.
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].
|Level|Shards; Required|Total; Shards|Total; SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|Ⅰ (1)|1|1|+1 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅱ (2)|1|2|+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅲ (3)|2|4|+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅳ (4)|2|6|+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅴ (5)|3|9|+5 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅵ (6)|3|12|+6 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅶ (7)|4|16|+7 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅷ (8)|4|20|+8 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅸ (9)|5|25|+9 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅹ (10)|7|32|+10 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
### Fusion
A Toucan Shard can be used in [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]] to create other [[Attributes|Shards]].

## Lore
When viewed in the [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] or [[Bazaar]], the Toucan Shard has the following description:
- Their beaks are surprisingly lightweight, made of a spongy, hollow bone structure that helps them stay agile.

# Transmission Tuner
Categories: Epic items, Items, Crafting ingredients, Slayer, Enderman Slayer, Mob Drops
[Transmission Tuner]
Type: Item
Rarity: EPIC ([[Rarity]])
Collection ([[Collections]]): Enderman Slayer III (3) ([[Enderman Slayer]])
Drop Chance: 5Rare ([[Drop Chance]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Rift Transferrable ([[Rift Dimension]]): ✔ Yes
[Shop]
Sell: 45,000 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: TRANSMISSION_TUNER
Tooltip: Combinable in Anvil; When applied to an item offering a Transmission ability, its ability range is increased by +1. Can be applied up to 4 times!; Right-click to view recipes!; X Rift-Transferable X EPIC
A Transmission Tuner is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Rift Dimension|# Rift-Transferable #]] item. It is unlocked at [[Enderman Slayer|Enderman Slayer III (3)]] and drops from the [[Voidgloom Seraph]].
## Obtaining
A Transmission Tuner is a [[Drop Chance|5Rare]] drop from Tier III+ [[Voidgloom Seraph|Voidgloom Seraphim]]. It is unlocked at [[Enderman Slayer|Enderman Slayer LVL III (3)]].
Drop Chances:
- Tier III: 2.24% (1/44.73)
- Tier IV: 2.12% (1/47.23)
## Usage
### Upgrading
A Transmission Tuner can be combined in an [[Anvil]] with any item with a Transmission ability, upgrading its range by 1 block. Up to [[Transmission Tuner]] 4 Transmission Tuners can be applied to an item, boosting its teleport range by up to 4 blocks.
The following items can have Transmission Tuners applied to them:
- [[Aspect of the End]]
- [[Aspect of the Void]]
- [[Etherwarp Conduit]]
- [[Sinseeker Scythe]]
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|Aspect of the Leech (Rare) ([[Aspect of the Leech]])|None|1x [[Aspect of the Leech]] [[Transmission Tuner]] 2x Transmission Tuner|Recipe: Transmission Tuner ×2 + [[Aspect of the Leech]] → [[Aspect of the Leech]]|
Note that the [[Aspect of the Leech|Aspect of the Leech (Uncommon)]] can only be upgraded once with Transmission Tuners: it can be further upgraded using 2 [[Super Leech Modifier|Super Leech Modifiers]].
## Trivia
- Despite Shadow Warp and Wither Impact teleporting the player, neither abilities can be upgraded with Transmission Tuners due to not having the word Transmission in their name.
- Transmission Tuners used to be bugged, boosting the range of Transmission abilities by 2 blocks: this also made it applicable only twice, up to 4 blocks.
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|May 4, 2023|Made Transmission Tuner # Rift-Transferable # ([[Rift Dimension]]).|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|May 31, 2021|0.11.5|Added Transmission Tuner.|
|Aug 1, 2022|Fixed Transmission Tuners applying twice.|
|Aug 1, 2022|Increased maximum Transmission Tuners able to be applied to items to 4.|
|Aug 18, 2022|0.14|Added Transmission Tuner to the [[Bazaar]].|
|Unknown Date|Fixed Transmission Tuner not working with the [[Sinseeker Scythe]].|
|Jun 20, 2023|0.19|Made Transmission Tuner # Rift-Transferable # ([[Rift Dimension]]).|

# Travel Scroll to Arachne's Sanctuary

Tooltip: Consume this item to add its destination to your Fast Travel options.; Requires MVP+ to consume!; Island: Spider's Den Teleport: Arachne's Sanctuary; EPIC TRAVEL SCROLL
The Travel Scroll to Arachne's Sanctuary is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Travel Scrolls|Travel Scroll]] that unlocks [[Fast Travel]] to [[Arachne's Sanctuary]].
## Obtaining
### Shop Purchase
The Travel Scroll to Arachne's Sanctuary can be purchased from the [[Spider Tamer]] for 5,000 (78 stacks plus 8) [[Spider Essence]].
## Usage
### Fast Travel
The Travel Scroll to Arachne's Sanctuary can be consumed if the player has 5[[Ranks|[MVP+]]] or higher to unlock the [[Fast Travel]] option to [[Arachne's Sanctuary]] in the [[Spider's Den]]. This includes access to /warp arachne and /warp sanctuary.
### Portalizer
The Travel Scroll to Arachne's Sanctuary can be combined with a [[Portalizer]] in an [[Anvil]] to create the [[Portals|Portal to Arachne's Sanctuary]].
## History

Categories: Epic items, Items in Sacks, Items, Mob Drops

[Shop]
Sell: 5,000 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: TREASURITE
Tooltip: Origin unknown.; EPIC
Treasurite is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] item that is used to forge items in [[The Forge]].
## Obtaining
### Mob Drops
|Level|Mob|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[Lv62]|[[Executive Sebastian]]|1|6Rare Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|1%|
|[Lv62]|[[Executive Viper]]|1|6Rare Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|1%|
|[Lv62]|[[Executive Wendy]]|1|6Rare Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|1%|
|[Lv54]|[[Grunt]]|1|6Rare Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|1%|
|[Lv61]|[[Treasure Hoarder]]|1|6Rare Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|0.5%|
### [[Treasure Chest|Treasure Chests]]
[[Treasurite]] 1 Treasurite has a 3.32% chance of rolling as a drop from common loot chests within [[Mithril Deposits]], and a 2.83% chance of dropping from rare loot chests within the [[Mithril Deposits]]. Also a rare chance for [[Treasurite]] 2 Treasurite from chests.
### [[Crystal Nucleus]]
[[Treasurite]] 5 Treasurite has a 0.66% chance to roll as a drop from the treasure stash obtained from placing all [[Gemstone Crystals]] down in the Crystal Nucleus. [[Treasurite]] 10 Treasurite has a 0.06% chance to be rolled as a drop.
### [[Upper Mines]]
[[Treasurite]] 1 Treasurite has a 2% (1 in 50) chance to replace any catch in the [[Upper Mines]] lake.
## Usage
Treasurite can be used to forge [[Drill Motor]] , [[Starfall Seasoning]] or [[Dwarven Geode]] in [[The Forge]]. 100 of it is also needed to forge the [[Jasper Drill X]].

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: TREECAPITATOR_AXE
Tooltip: Sweep: +25; Gemstones: [] []; Ability: Throwing Axe RIGHT CLICK While on Foraging Islands, throw your Axe at a tree! It will sweep 50% of the logs it normally would!; Cooldown: 1s; Ability: Park Enthusiast Gain +100 Foraging Fortune while in The Park.; This item can be reforged!; EPIC AXE
The Treecapitator is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Axes|Axe]]. It is unlocked at [[Jungle Log|Jungle Log VII (7)]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Jungle Log VII (7) ([[Jungle Log]])|1x [[Spruce Axe]] 32x [[Enchanted Jungle Log]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Jungle Log]] x4 ×8 + [[Spruce Axe]] → Treecapitator|
## Usage
Axe:
Treecapitator [[Sweep]]: +25
[Effects]
[Ability: Throwing Axe RIGHT CLICK; While on Foraging Islands, throw your Axe at a tree! It will sweep 50% of the logs it normally would!; Cooldown: 1s Ability: Park Enthusiast; Gain +100 Foraging Fortune while in The Park.]
### Crafting
On [[Stranded|☀ Stranded]], the Treecapitator has the following recipe:
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Stranded Treecapitator]]|None|[[Treecapitator]] 1x Treecapitator|Recipe: Treecapitator → [[Stranded Treecapitator]]|
## Tips
- If chopping trees in [[The Park]], [[Haste Potion|Haste III]] allows an [[Efficiency|Efficiency V]] Treecapitator to instantly break wood.
  - A [[Silverfish Pet]] can be used to achieve this.
  - A [[God Potion]] also grants [[Haste Potion|Haste IV]].
  - Due to [[Galatea|Galatea's]] custom block mechanics, [[Haste Potion|Haste]] does nothing there.
- [Lv100] [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Monkey Pet]] increases player's [[Sweep]] while on [[The Park]] by 10 , allowing players to reach the 35 [[Sweep]] that is required to destroy the maximum amount of wood.
- Players should prioritize big trees over the log piles. Log piles have only about 25-30 logs, but big trees almost always have 35.
- [[Hot Potato Book|Hot Potato Books]] and [[Fuming Potato Book|Fuming Potato Books]] can be applied to grant extra [[Strength]] for the [[Heart of the Forest|Forest Strength]] perk at [[Heart of the Forest|Heart of the Forest VI]], increasing [[Sweep]]
## Trivia
- The Treecapitator is named after the classic mod, Treecapitator.
  - It also references the Lumberjack Axe's ability in another [[Hypixel]] minigame, UHC Champions.
## History

|Jun 24, 2026|Added a custom texture for Treecapitator as part of the SkyBlock Resource Pack.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: TUNGSTEN_KEY
Tooltip: Can be used to open backpacks with a Tungsten lock held by Frozen Corpses in the Glacite Mineshafts.; EPIC
Tungsten Key is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] item unlocked at [[Tungsten|Tungsten IV (4)]].
## Obtaining
Tungsten Key can be forged at [[The Forge]] with 192 (3 stacks) [[Enchanted Tungsten]] and 1 [[Bejeweled Handle]]. It takes 30 seconds to forge. It requires [[Tungsten|Tungsten IV (4)]] and [[Heart of the Mountain|Heart of the Mountain VII (7)]] to forge.
Tungsten Key can also be obtained as a MINOR reward from [[Raffle of the Century]], or from the [[Gift Bag of the Century]].
### Forging Tree
- [[Tungsten Key]] 1 Tungsten Key
  - 192 [[Enchanted Tungsten]] 30,720 (480 stacks) [[Tungsten]]
  - 1 [[Bejeweled Handle]] 3 [[Glacite Jewel]]
### Frozen Corpses
Tungsten Keys can be obtained from opening [[Frozen Corpses]].
|Type|Quantity|Chance Per Roll|
|-|-|-|
|Lapis|[[Tungsten Key]] 1 Tungsten Key|0.15% (1 in 670)|
|Umber Tungsten|[[Tungsten Key]] 1 Tungsten Key|0.15% (1 in 654)|
|Vanguard|[[Tungsten Key]] 1 Tungsten Key|2.28% (1 in 43.94)|
|Vanguard|[[Tungsten Key]] 2 Tungsten Key|1.14% (1 in 87.88)|
|Vanguard|[[Tungsten Key]] 4 Tungsten Key|0.57% (1 in 175.75)|
## Usage
Tungsten Key can be used in [[Glacite Mineshafts]] to open [[Frozen Corpses|Frozen Corpse]] backpacks that have a Tungsten lock. The item will be consumed after use.

|Lapis|[[Umber Key]] 1 Umber Key|0.15% (1 in 670)|
|Umber Tungsten|[[Umber Key]] 1 Umber Key|0.15% (1 in 654)|

|Vanguard|[[Umber Key]] 2 Umber Key|1.14% (1 in 87.88)|

## Usage
An Umber Key can be used in [[Glacite Mineshafts]] to open [[Frozen Corpses|Frozen Corpse]] backpacks that have an Umber lock. The item will be consumed after use.

# Unalloyed Speed
Aliases: Unalloy
Categories: Epic items, Items, Craftable items, Pet items
[Unalloyed Speed]
Type: Pet Item ([[Pet Items]])
Rarity: EPIC ([[Rarity]])
Uses: Grants +50 max [[Speed]] cap
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Sell: 150,000 [[Coins]]
[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 81,920x (1,280 stacks (81,920 items total)) [[Sugar Cane]] 32x [[Cane Knot]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: UNALLOYED_SPEED
Tooltip: Consumed on use; Pet Items can boost pets in many powerful ways! A pet can only hold one Pet Item, but you can swap it at any time!; The pet must be visible to apply the item!; Grants +50 Max Speed Cap.; Right-click on your summoned pet to give it this item!; EPIC PET ITEM
Unalloyed Speed is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Pet Items|Pet Item]] that increases the player's [[Speed]] cap by +50 .
## Usage
Right-clicking a [[Pets|Pet]] whilst holding a Unalloyed Speed will equip it to the [[Pets|Pet]], increasing the player's [[Speed]] cap by +50 . If another [[Pet Items|Pet Item]] were equipped on the [[Pets|Pet]], it would refund the Unalloyed Speed for 250,000 [[Coins]] and remove the perks it gave.
Unalloyed Speed cannot be applied to a [[Snail Pet]].
## Game Messages
|Condition|Associated Text|
|-|-|
|Attempting to use it on [[Snail Pet]]|This item is not compatible with your pet!|
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|32x [[Cane Knot]] 512x (8 stacks (512 items total)) [[Enchanted Sugar]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Sugar]] x64 ×8 + [[Cane Knot]] x32 → Unalloyed Speed|
## Trivia
- [[Unalloyed Speed]] Unalloyed Speed is the only Pet Item that is on Bazaar, rather than being on [[Auction House]].
- [[Unalloyed Speed]] Unalloyed Speed is incompatible with all rarities of [[Snail Pet]], despite its [[Speed]] limiting ability, Slow and Steady being exclusive to [[Rarity|RARE]] rarities and higher.
- [[Unalloyed Speed]] Unalloyed Speed is the only pet item that directly increases a cap of a stat.
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Mar 31, 2026|Added Unalloyed Speed.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Apr 28, 2026|0.24.4|Added Unalloyed Speed.|

The Validus Glyph is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] Quest Item obtained in the [[Rift Dimension]].
## Obtaining
The Validus Glyph can be obtained by starting [[Sorcerer Okron]]'s quest and then opening the chest at [[Coordinates|-57.5, 70, -95.5]] by the pond in the [[West Village]]. Once given to Okron, it can no longer be obtained.
## Usage
The Validus Glyph can be given to [[Sorcerer Okron]]. When given, he will task the player with using the [[Warding Diamathame]] to imbue a spell onto the house.

Next →: None
[Item Properties]

[Shop]
Sell: 888,025 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: VANQUISHED_BLAZE_BELT; VANQUISHED_GHAST_CLOAK; VANQUISHED_MAGMA_NECKLACE; VANQUISHED_GLOWSTONE_GAUNTLET
Tooltip: Defense: +20; Strength: +5; Ability: Blazing Palisade Gain a 20% chance to reflect Fireballs.; Piece Bonus: Blaze Consumer Kill Blazes to accumulate  Strength.; Piece Bonus: +0 Next Upgrade: +1 (0\ 1,000); Right-click to equip!; This item can be reforged!; ❣ Requires Combat Skill 22.; EPIC BELT
Vanquisher Set is a group of four different [[Equipment]] pieces, and the upgraded set of the [[Crimson Hunter Set]]: [[Vanquished Blaze Belt]], [[Vanquished Ghast Cloak]], [[Vanquished Magma Necklace]], and [[Vanquished Glowstone Gauntlet]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Vanquished Blaze Belt]]|Blaze Rod VII (7) ([[Blaze Rod]])|1x [[Blaze Belt]] 2x [[Enchanted Blaze Rod]] 3x [[Nether Star]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Blaze Rod]] ×2 + [[Blaze Belt]] + [[Nether Star]] ×3 → [[Vanquished Blaze Belt]]|
|[[Vanquished Ghast Cloak]]|Ghast Tear VII (7) ([[Ghast Tear]])|1x [[Ghast Cloak]] 3x [[Nether Star]] 256x (4 stacks (256 items total)) [[Silver Fang]]|Recipe: [[Nether Star]] ×3 + [[Silver Fang]] x64 ×4 + [[Ghast Cloak]] → [[Vanquished Ghast Cloak]]|
|[[Vanquished Magma Necklace]]|Magma Cream VII (7) ([[Magma Cream]])|1x [[Magma Necklace]] 3x [[Nether Star]] 256x [[Enchanted Magma Cream]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Magma Cream]] x64 ×4 + [[Magma Necklace]] + [[Nether Star]] x3 → [[Vanquished Magma Necklace]]|
|[[Vanquished Glowstone Gauntlet]]|Glowstone Dust VII (7) ([[Glowstone Dust]])|1x [[Glowstone Gauntlet]] 3x [[Nether Star]] 4x [[Enchanted Glowstone]]|Recipe: [[Nether Star]] ×3 + [[Enchanted Glowstone]] ×4 + [[Glowstone Gauntlet]] → [[Vanquished Glowstone Gauntlet]]|
## Usage
Armor Set:
|Belt|Cloak|
|-|-|
|[[Vanquished Blaze Belt]]; [[Defense]]: +20 [[Strength]]: +5 Ability: Blazing Palisade; Gain a 20% chance to reflect Fireballs. Piece Bonus: Blaze Consumer; Kill Blazes to accumulate Strength.|[[Vanquished Ghast Cloak]]; [[Health]]: +10 [[Defense]]: +10 Ability: Blazing Restoration; Restores +15 [[Health]] every second while worn. Piece Bonus: Ghast Blaster; Kill Ghasts to accumulate Defense.|
|Necklace|Gloves|
|[[Vanquished Magma Necklace]]; [[Health]]: +20 Ability: Cube Fanatic; Gain 20% more Magma Cream when killing Magma Cubes. Piece Bonus: Magma Cube Absorber; Kill Magma Cubes to accumulate [[Crit Damage]].|[[Vanquished Glowstone Gauntlet]]; [[Intelligence]]: +15 [[Mining Fortune]]: +7 Piece Bonus: Glowing; Mine Glowstone to accumulate Intelligence.|
[Total]
[Vanquisher Set Health: +30 Defense: +30 Strength: +5 Intelligence: +15 Mining Fortune: +7]
[Effects]
[Secret Full Set Bonus: Vanquisher Set; While on the Crimson Isle, receive -10% damage in combat, increase your damage by 1.1x , and gain +10 Vitality.]
Each of the pieces has its own Piece Bonus, that requires doing a specific task, and in return it grants additional stats to the player when the item is worn. The abilities have 10 tiers, each requiring 1,000 actions. Since the stats aren't granted to the item, they are not affected by [[Star Upgrades]]. In total the full set of equipment can grant up to 10 [[Crit Damage]], 10 [[Defense]], 10 [[Strength]] and 10 [[Intelligence]].
## Notes
- It is important to note that the Stats from this equipment's Piece Bonuses doesn't show in the items' stats, which might give the false impression of it giving up to fewer stats less than they actually give.
## Trivia
- Despite all the pieces being named Vanquished, the set bonus and the set itself is named after the mob [[Vanquisher]].
- Despite [[Blaze Pet]]'s Nether Embodiment perk working on both [[Crimson Isle]] and [[Kuudra]], Vanquisher Set does not work inside of [[Kuudra]].
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Feb 22, 2022|Added Vanquisher Set.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Apr 20, 2022|0.13|Added Vanquisher Set.|
|Jun 23, 2025|0.23|Added 10 [[Vitality]] to the Full Set Bonus.|

Tooltip: Damage: +250; Strength: +100; Fires a volley of 5 arrows. Arrows apply venom to all enemies hit dealing 0 Damage every second for 3; seconds.; This item is Arachnal !; This item can be reforged!; EPIC BOW
The Venom's Touch is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Bows|Bow]]. It fires 5 [[Arrows]] from the player. When these arrows hit, they apply a venom which deals equal to a player's [[Intelligence]] multiplied by 2, lasting for 3 seconds.
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|10x [[Enchanted Spider Eye]] 48x [[Soul String]] 120x (1 stack plus 56 (120 in total)) [[Enchanted Red Mushroom]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Red Mushroom]] x60 ×2 + [[Soul String]] x16 ×3 + [[Enchanted Spider Eye]] x10 → Venom's Touch|
|None|16x [[Enchanted Spider Eye]] 48x [[Soul String]] 120x [[Enchanted Brown Mushroom]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Brown Mushroom]] x60 ×2 + [[Soul String]] x16 ×3 + [[Enchanted Spider Eye]] x16 → Venom's Touch|
## Usage
Bow:
Venom's Touch [[Damage]]: +250 [[Strength]]: +100
[Effects]
[Fires a volley of 5 arrows. Arrows apply venom to all enemies hit dealing <mana * 2> Damage every second for 3 seconds.]
Bow:
Venom's Touch [[Damage]]: +370 [[Strength]]: +180
[Effects]
[Fires a volley of 5 arrows. Arrows apply venom to all enemies hit dealing <mana * 2> Damage every second for 3 seconds.]
## Upgrading
The Venom's Touch can be upgraded, buffing its [[Damage]] by 120 and its [[Strength]] by 80.
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|[[Venom's Touch]] 1x Venom's Touch 2x [[Dark Queen's Soul]] 128x (2 stacks (128 items total)) [[Enchanted Spider Eye]] 192x [[Soul String]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Spider Eye]] x64 ×2 + [[Soul String]] x64 ×3 + [[Dark Queen's Soul]] x2 + Venom's Touch → [[Venom's Touch]]|
## Tips
- When crafting the Bow, the player can use [[Enchanted Brown Mushroom]] instead of [[Enchanted Red Mushroom]], which may end up being cheaper if the materials are bought from the [[Bazaar]]. Players must use the same type of mushroom for both stacks in the recipe.
## Trivia
- When the update was first released, the ability could be used on other players.
- Venom's Touch cannot have the [[Dragon Tracer]] [[Enchantments|Enchantment]] applied to it.

Aliases: Venom, Vial Of Venom
Categories: Epic items, Items, Permanent Consumables

Type: Item
Rarity: EPIC ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: VIAL_OF_VENOM
Tooltip: Consumable; Consume this item to permanently gain +1 Crit Damage.; You can only consume 5 of this item before the damage to your internal organs becomes too extensive to drink more.; Right-click to consume!; EPIC
A Vial of Venom is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Consumables|Permanent Consumable]] item used to increase the player's [[Crit Damage]].
## Obtaining
A Vial of Venom has a 0.5% (1 in 200) chance to drop from a Tier IV or above [[Tarantula Broodfather]] once at [[Spider Slayer|Spider Slayer VI (6)]].
## Usage
Consuming a Vial of Venom grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+2 SkyBlock XP]] and a permanent +1 [[Crit Damage]]. Up to 5 Vials of Venom can be consumed in total.
## Game Message
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|First Vial|YIPPEE! The venom stings as it trickles down your throat. Immunity here you come!|
|Second Vial|GULP! The venom is viscous and hard to swallow. Was this a good idea?|
|Third Vial|BLEGH! The taste just keeps getting worse and worse. Whose idea was this, again?|
|Fourth Vial|CHUG! You finish the vial in a single swig. You can feel your insides disintegrating.|
|Fifth Vial|NOPE! No...more...organs...ouchie.|
|Attempting to drink more than 5|You've already consumed the maximum amount of this item!|
## History

[SkyBlock Prototype]

The Vis Glyph is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] Quest Item obtained in the [[Rift Dimension]].
## Obtaining
The Vis Glyph can be obtained after starting [[Sorcerer Okron]]'s quest and imbuing the [[Conclamatus Glyph]], then opening the chest at [[Coordinates|-108.5, 72, -99.5]] by [[Joey McPizza]] and [[Chef]] within the [[West Village]]. Once given to Okron, it can no longer be obtained.
## Usage
The Vis Glyph can be given to [[Sorcerer Okron]]. When given, he will task the player with using the [[Warding Diamathame]] to imbue a spell onto the house.

Tooltip: Damage: +190; Strength: +80; Crit Damage: +30%; Intelligence: +200; Gemstones: [] []; Deal +250% damage to Endermen.; Receive 9% less damage from Endermen when held.; Gain +30☯ Combat Wisdom against Endermen.; Ability: Soulcry RIGHT CLICK Gain +300 Ferocity against Endermen for 4s Soulflow Cost: 1; Mana Cost: 200 Cooldown: 4s; This item can be reforged!; ☠ Requires Enderman Slayer 5.; EPIC SWORD
The Vorpal Katana is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Swords|Sword]]. It is unlocked at [[Enderman Slayer|Enderman Slayer V (5)]]. It is the upgraded form of the [[Voidedge Katana]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Enderman Slayer V (5) ([[Enderman Slayer]])|1x [[Null Edge]] 1x [[Voidedge Katana]] 8x [[Null Ovoid]] 8x [[Refined Mithril]]|Recipe: [[Null Edge]] + [[Null Ovoid]] x8 + [[Refined Mithril]] x4 ×2 + [[Voidedge Katana]] → Vorpal Katana|
## Usage
Sword:
Vorpal Katana [[Damage]]: +190 [[Strength]]: +80 [[Crit Damage]]: +30% [[Intelligence]]: +200
[Effects]
[Deal +250% damage to Endermen. Receive 9% less damage from Endermen when held. Gain +30 Combat Wisdom against Endermen. Ability: Soulcry RIGHT CLICK; Gain +300 Ferocity against Endermen for 4s.; Soulflow Cost (Soulflow (Mechanic)): 1; Mana Cost (Intelligence): 200; Cooldown: 4s]
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Atomsplit Katana]]|Enderman Slayer VI (6) ([[Enderman Slayer]])|[[Vorpal Katana]] 1x Vorpal Katana 2x [[Null Blade]]|Recipe: [[Null Blade]] ×2 + Vorpal Katana → [[Atomsplit Katana]]|
## Trivia
- The Vorpal Katana's name references the 1871 nonsense poem "Jabberwocky" by Lewis Carroll, in which a character uses a "vorpal" sword (a made-up adjective) to slay a powerful beast.
  - The word "vorpal" is onomatopoeic for the sounds Endermen make, which is likely why this name was chosen, as it is an end-themed weapon.

# Wand of Restoration
Aliases: Wand Of Restoration
Categories: Epic items, Items, Craftable items, Crafting ingredients, Slayer, Zombie Slayer, Wands, Healing Wand, Support Items
[Wand of Restoration]
Type: Wand ([[Wands]])
Rarity: EPIC ([[Rarity]])
Slayer Level ([[Slayer]]) Requirement: Zombie Slayer VI (6) ([[Zombie Slayer]])
[Upgrades]
← Previous: [[Wand of Mending]]
Next →: [[Wand of Atonement]]
[Item Properties]

Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✔ Yes (+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]]))
[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 87,040x (1,360 stacks (87,040 items total)) [[String]] 12,160x (190 stacks (12,160 items total)) [[Dark Oak Log]] 2,192x (34 stacks plus 16 (2,192 in total)) [[Revenant Flesh]] 1x [[Stick]]
Bazaar Material cost (Cost to buy all necessary materials from the Bazaar.): (not including: 1 [[Stick]])
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: WAND_OF_RESTORATION
Tooltip: Ability: Big Heal RIGHT CLICK Heal 100❤ per second for 6s.; Wand heals don't stack.; Vitality Cost: 25 Cooldown: 1s; ☠ Requires Zombie Slayer 6.; EPIC WAND
The Wand of Restoration is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Wands|Healing Wand]] and [[Support Items|Support Item]] unlocked at [[Zombie Slayer|Zombie Slayer VI (6)]]. It is an upgraded version of the [[Wand of Mending]] and can be upgraded into the [[Wand of Atonement]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Zombie Slayer VI (6) ([[Zombie Slayer]])|1x [[Wand of Mending]] 16x [[Revenant Viscera]] 64x [[Enchanted Dark Oak Log]]|Recipe: [[Revenant Viscera]] x16 + [[Wand of Mending]] + [[Enchanted Dark Oak Log]] x64 → Wand of Restoration|
## Usage
Wand:
Wand of Restoration
[Effects]
[Ability: Big Heal RIGHT CLICK; Heal 100 Health/s for 6s.; Wand heals don't stack.; Vitality Cost (Vitality): 25; Cooldown: 1s]
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Wand of Atonement]]|Zombie Slayer VII (7) ([[Zombie Slayer]])|[[Wand of Restoration]] 1x Wand of Restoration 64x [[Revenant Viscera]] 64x [[Enchanted Dark Oak Log]]|Recipe: [[Revenant Viscera]] x64 + Wand of Restoration + [[Enchanted Dark Oak Log]] x64 → [[Wand of Atonement]]|
## Tips
- The set bonuses of [[Revenant Armor]] and [[Reaper Armor]] increase healing from [[Wands|Healing Wands]] by 50%.
- Each armor piece enchanted with [[Habanero Tactics|Habanero Tactics V]] grants 7.5% additional healing from [[Wands|Healing Wands]].
## Trivia
- Although consecutive casts do not stack, the first healing tick is applied immediately. With enough [[Vitality]], repeatedly casting the ability can heal 200 [[Health]] per second.

|Oct 14, 2019|Fixed the +50% Healing Wand bonus from [[Revenant Armor]].|

## Trivia
- While this item requires [[Combat|Combat XXII (22)]], its ability is quite underwhelming. This is due to items such as the [[Weird Tuba]], which gives better stats without costing health, or [[Deployables]] which grant various stats that are often more useful, making this item close to useless. However, this item can be stacked with the aforementioned items if one truly needs the extra [[Strength]].
  - However, it does stack with both and can be useful for onetime damage boost which can help when doing some [[Bingo]] goals

# Warped Stone
Aliases: Warped
Categories: Epic items, Reforge stones, Catacombs Loot, Dungeons, Reforging
If you look deep inside, you risk finding yourself somewhere else.
[Warped Stone]
Type: Reforge Stone ([[Reforge Stones]])
Rarity: EPIC ([[Rarity]])
Tooltip Text: If you look deep inside, you risk finding yourself somewhere else.
[Reforge/Power Stone]
Reforge: Warped
Applied To: [[Aspect of the End]] [[Aspect of the Void]]
Apply Cost: 250,000 [[Coins]]
Req. Skill Level: Mining XXVI (26) ([[Mining]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Sell: 1 Coin ([[Coins]])
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: AOTE_STONE
Tooltip: Reforge Stone Combinable in Anvil; Applies the Warped reforge when combined with Aspect of the End and Aspect of the Void.; If you look deep inside, you risk finding yourself somewhere else.; Grants the following stats that scale with the item's rarity:;  Damage  Strength  Intelligence; Right-click to view rarity scaling!; ❣ Requires Mining Skill 26.; EPIC REFORGE STONE
  Not to be confused with [[End Stone Geode]].
The Warped Stone is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Reforge Stones|Reforge Stone]] that applies the [[Reforging|Warped]] [[Reforging|Reforge]] to an [[Aspect of the End]] and [[Aspect of the Void]]. It grants [[Damage]], [[Strength]], and [[Intelligence]].
## Obtaining
### Dungeon Reward Chests
The Warped Stone can be dropped from [[Dungeon Reward Chest|Dungeon Reward Chests]] on [[The Catacombs - Floor V]].
|Catacombs Floor ([[Catacombs]])|Chest ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]])|Cost; (Chest + Added)|Average Chance; (No Bonuses)|Average Chance; (S+ (Score above 300 points.) ([[Dungeon Score]]) Max Bonuses ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]]))|Quality ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]])|Weight ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]])|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|Floor V ([[The Catacombs - Floor V]])|Emerald|5,000,000 [[Coins]]|0.0575% (1 in 1739 runs)|0.0575% (1 in 1738 runs)|260|1|
|Floor V ([[The Catacombs - Floor V]])|Obsidian|5,000,000 [[Coins]]|0.2959% (1 in 338 runs)|0.5023% (1 in 199.1 runs)|260|1|
|Floor V ([[The Catacombs - Floor V]])|Bedrock|5,000,000 [[Coins]]|0.8081% (1 in 123.7 runs)|1.0269% (1 in 97.4 runs)|270|2|
|Floor V ([[The Catacombs - Floor V]]) (Master Mode)|Emerald|5,000,000 [[Coins]]|0.0575% (1 in 1738 runs)|0.065% (1 in 1537.8 runs)|260|1|
|Floor V ([[The Catacombs - Floor V]]) (Master Mode)|Obsidian|5,000,000 [[Coins]]|0.2933% (1 in 341 runs)|0.5668% (1 in 176.4 runs)|260|1|
|Floor V ([[The Catacombs - Floor V]]) (Master Mode)|Bedrock|5,000,000 [[Coins]]|0.9465% (1 in 105.7 runs)|1.3556% (1 in 73.8 runs)|270|2|
## Usage
### Reforging
Warped Stone can be combined with an [[Aspect of the End]] or [[Aspect of the Void]] using an [[Anvil]] or [[The Hex]] to apply the [[Reforging|Warped]] reforge, to grant [[Intelligence]], which scales with the Sword's rarity and 165 [[Damage]], 165 [[Strength]]. This has a skill requirement of [[Mining|Mining XXVI (26)]]. A Sword that is already reforged to [[Reforging|Warped]] will grant the stats, even if the player doesn't meet the requirement to apply it.
|Stat Bonus|Rarity COMMON ([[Rarity]])|Rarity UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|Rarity RARE ([[Rarity]])|Rarity EPIC ([[Rarity]])|Rarity LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|Rarity MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|Stat Bonus|+165 Dmg ([[Damage]]) +165 Str ([[Strength]])|+165 Dmg ([[Damage]]) +165 Str ([[Strength]])|+165 Dmg ([[Damage]]) +65 Int ([[Intelligence]]) +165 Str ([[Strength]])|+165 Dmg ([[Damage]]) +100 Int ([[Intelligence]]) +165 Str ([[Strength]])|+165 Dmg ([[Damage]]) +150 Int ([[Intelligence]]) +165 Str ([[Strength]])|+165 Dmg ([[Damage]]) +150 Int ([[Intelligence]]) +165 Str ([[Strength]])|
|Cost to apply|250,000 [[Coins]]|250,000 [[Coins]]|250,000 [[Coins]]|250,000 [[Coins]]|250,000 [[Coins]]|250,000 [[Coins]]|
## Trivia
- It was suspected to potentially be applicable to any melee weapon with a movement ability.
  - On the day of release, a faked image of a [[Reforging|Warped]] [[Shadow Fury]] spread among the community.
    - This has been debunked by a Hypixel admin.
- The reforge used to permanently convert the weapon into [[Dungeons|Dungeon gear]], even if the reforge is replaced. This means it was affected by [[Catacombs]] level, but did not have a [[Catacombs]] requirement and could not be [[Star Upgrades|Star Upgraded]].
  - It was the only reforge in the game to have this effect, it is unknown when exactly this behavior was removed, and if the change was intentional or not.
- It and the [[Midas Jewel]] are the only [[Reforge Stones|Reforge Stone]] that have no stat at [[Rarity|DIVINE]] rarity.

# Warty
Categories: Epic items, Items
[Warty]
Type: Item
Rarity: EPIC ([[Rarity]])
Obtained: Farming [[Nether Wart]] with Wart Eater ([[Attributes]]) active
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Sell: 500 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: WARTY
Tooltip: Bring 3,000 of these to Carrolyn in Scarleton on the Crimson Isle to permanently gain +5 Health and +12 Nether Wart Fortune.; EPIC
A Warty is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] item.
## Obtaining
Warty has a 0.01% chance per [[Attributes|Wart Eater]] ( [[Wartybug Shard]]) attribute level (up to 0.1%) to drop each time a mature [[Nether Wart]] crop is harvested by the player. This chance can also be increased by the [[Overbloom]] stat.
## Usage
The player can bring [[Warty]] 3,000 (46 stacks plus 56) Warty to [[Carrolyn]] in [[Scarleton]] to gain a permanent +5 [[Health]] and +12 [[Crop Fortune|Nether Wart Fortune]].
## Trivia
- With the [[Attributes|Wart Eater]] attribute maxed and 0 [[Overbloom]], it takes an average of 41 hours and 40 minutes of farming Nether Wart at 20 BPS (Blocks Per Second) to obtain [[Warty]] 3,000 Warty.

A Washed-up Souvenir is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Pet Items|Pet Item]] that grants +5 [[Sea Creature Chance]].
## Obtaining
### Mob Drops
|Level|Mob|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[Lv208]|Empyrean Stranded Nymph ([[Stranded Nymph]])|1|6Rare Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|0.5%|
|[Lv178]|Exalted Stranded Nymph ([[Stranded Nymph]])|1|6Rare Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|0.4%|
|[Lv128]|Venerable Stranded Nymph ([[Stranded Nymph]])|1|6Rare Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|0.3%|
|[Lv83]|Stalwart Stranded Nymph ([[Stranded Nymph]])|1|6Rare Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|0.25%|
|[Lv44]|Blessed Stranded Nymph ([[Stranded Nymph]])|1|6Rare Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|0.2%|
## Usage
Right-clicking a [[Pets|Pet]] whilst holding a Washed-up Souvenir will equip it to the [[Pets|Pet]], granting it +5 [[Sea Creature Chance]]. If another [[Pet Items|Pet Item]] were equipped on the [[Pets|Pet]], it would refund the Washed-up Souvenir for 250,000 [[Coins]] and remove the perks it gave.

The Water Hydra Shard is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Fishing Speed]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Hunting
A Water Hydra Shard can be obtained from killing a [[Water Hydra]]. Since it is an [[Elusive]] mob, it is guaranteed to be [[Charm Chance|Charmed]] and drop one.
### Fusion
A Water Hydra Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|ID Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]].
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Water Hydra Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Fishing Speed]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Fishing Speed ([[Attributes]]); Grants +3–30 [[Fishing Speed]].
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].
|Level|Shards; Required|Total; Shards|Total; SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|Ⅰ (1)|1|1|+1 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅱ (2)|1|2|+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅲ (3)|2|4|+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅳ (4)|2|6|+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅴ (5)|3|9|+5 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅵ (6)|3|12|+6 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅶ (7)|4|16|+7 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅷ (8)|4|20|+8 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅸ (9)|5|25|+9 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅹ (10)|7|32|+10 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
### Fusion
A Water Hydra Shard can be used in [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]] to create other [[Attributes|Shards]].
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
[Data from SkyShards.]
## Lore
When viewed in the [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] or [[Bazaar]], the Water Hydra Shard has the following description:
- Their scales shimmer with a iridescent blue-green hue, blending seamlessly with the deep waters they call home.
## Trivia
- Despite being an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Attributes|Shard]], the Water Hydra Shard does not have a [[Hunting]] level requirement.

[Ability: Howl RIGHT CLICK; You and 4 nearby players gain: +40 Strength +5 Attack Speed +30 Speed +10 Crit Damage for 20 seconds.; Effect doesn't stack.; Mana Cost (Intelligence): 150; Cooldown: 20s]
Due to it being [[Rift Dimension|# Rift-Transferable #]], the Weirder Tuba can be transferred to the [[Hub Island|Hub]] through the Ender Chest next to [[Inverted Sirius]]. Once in the [[Hub Island|Hub]], the Weirder Tuba will grant different stats when used, and will have an increased [[Mana|Mana Cost]].

A Winter Crystal is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Floating Crystals|Floating Crystal]].
## Obtaining
Winter Crystal spawns on [[Winter Island (Item)]] placement by the player on their Private Island.
## Usage
When placed on the [[Private Island]], a Winter Crystal increases the speed of all [[Snow Minion|Snow Minions]] and [[Ice Minion|Ice Minions]] by 5%, and spawns a [[Gifts|Gift]] ( [[White Gift|White]], [[Green Gift|Green]] or [[Red Gift|Red]]) once every [[Time Systems|SkyBlock Year]] in a 15 block radius. Only 1 Winter Crystal can be placed at once.

# Wither Artifact
Categories: Epic items, Accessories, Crafting ingredients, Dark Auction, Artifact
[Wither Artifact]
Type: Accessory ([[Accessories]])
Rarity: EPIC ([[Rarity]])
[Upgrades]
← Previous: None
Next →: [[Wither Relic]]
[Item Properties]
Upgradeable: ✔ Yes
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Sell: 1,000 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: WITHER_ARTIFACT
Tooltip: Accessory Power: +12; Reduces the damage taken from  Wither mobs by 20%.; Works while in Accessory Bag!; EPIC ACCESSORY
The Wither Artifact is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Accessories|Accessory]]. It is acquired through the [[Dark Auction]]. It reduces the damage taken from [[Wither]] mobs by 20% and can be upgraded to a [[Wither Relic]].
## Obtaining
The Wither Artifact can be purchased from the [[Dark Auction]] Rounds 1, 3, or 4 with starting bid of 10,000,000 [[Coins]]. It has an overall 32.477% chance to appear.
Wither Artifact can also be obtained as a MEDIUM reward from [[Raffle of the Century]].
## Usage
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Wither Relic]]|None|[[Wither Artifact]] 1x Wither Artifact 8x [[Wither Catalyst]]|Recipe: [[Wither Catalyst]] ×8 + Wither Artifact → [[Wither Relic]]|
## Tips
Since the Wither Artifact is acquired from the [[Dark Auction]], the price may fluctuate due to supply and demand. If no one at the auction has an interest in the item, the player may be able to bid significantly less than someone in a different lobby where multiple players desire the item. This appears to be a popular end game accessory because of the damage reduction against withers, which includes [[Necron]].
## Trivia
- There was a bug where the Wither Artifact stacked with the [[Wither Relic]] to get 45% reduced damage taken from Withers.
  - This has been patched as of 2021 October 5.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Dec 3, 2019|Added Wither Artifact.|
|Oct 5, 2021|Fixed Wither Artifact and [[Wither Relic]] stacking.|
|Aug 5, 2025|0.23.3|Changed damage reduction to affect the [[Wither]] mob type.|
|Jan 22, 2026|Increased Wither Artifact starting price from 50,000 [[Coins]] to 10,000,000 [[Coins]].|

Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Sell: 1 Coin ([[Coins]])
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: WITHER_BLOOD
Tooltip: Reforge Stone Combinable in Anvil; Applies the Withered reforge when combined with a Melee Weapon.; Grants the following stats that scale with the item's rarity:;  Strength; Withered Bonus Grants +1 Strength per Catacombs skill level you have.; Right-click to view rarity scaling!; ❣ Requires Mining Skill 30.; EPIC REFORGE STONE
The Wither Blood is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Reforge Stones|Reforge Stone]], which can apply the [[Reforging|Withered]] [[Reforging|Reforge]] to a [[Weapons|melee weapon]], granting [[Strength]].
## Obtaining
### Boss Collection
Wither Blood can be claimed once as a reward from [[Necron|Necron I (1)]], which is unlocked after killing [[Necron]] 50 times.
### Dungeon Reward Chests
Wither Blood can be obtained from [[Dungeon Reward Chest|Dungeon Reward Chests]] in [[The Catacombs - Floor VII]].
|Catacombs Floor ([[Catacombs]])|Chest ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]])|Cost; (Chest + Added)|Average Chance; (No Bonuses)|Average Chance; (S+ (Score above 300 points.) ([[Dungeon Score]]) Max Bonuses ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]]))|Quality ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]])|Weight ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]])|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|Floor VII ([[The Catacombs - Floor VII]])|Emerald|2,500,000 [[Coins]]|1.0811% (1 in 92.5 runs)|2.0336% (1 in 49.2 runs)|210|2|
|Floor VII ([[The Catacombs - Floor VII]])|Obsidian|2,500,000 [[Coins]]|3.4168% (1 in 29.3 runs)|4.5296% (1 in 22.1 runs)|210|6|
|Floor VII ([[The Catacombs - Floor VII]])|Bedrock|3,000,000 [[Coins]]|6.2826% (1 in 15.9 runs)|8.114% (1 in 12.3 runs)|210|480|
|Floor VII ([[The Catacombs - Floor VII]]) (Master Mode)|Emerald|2,500,000 [[Coins]]|1.4431% (1 in 69.3 runs)|2.0753% (1 in 48.2 runs)|210|2|
|Floor VII ([[The Catacombs - Floor VII]]) (Master Mode)|Obsidian|2,500,000 [[Coins]]|1.251% (1 in 79.9 runs)|2.4389% (1 in 41 runs)|210|6|
|Floor VII ([[The Catacombs - Floor VII]]) (Master Mode)|Bedrock|3,000,000 [[Coins]]|7.4413% (1 in 13.4 runs)|9.7425% (1 in 10.3 runs)|210|480|
## Usage
### Reforging
Wither Blood can be applied to [[Swords]], [[Longswords]] and [[Gemstone Gauntlet]] using an [[Anvil]] or [[The Hex]] to apply the [[Reforging|Withered]] reforge, to grant [[Strength]], which scales with the weapon's rarity. This has a skill requirement of [[Mining|Mining XXX (30)]]. A weapon that is already reforged to [[Reforging|Withered]] will grant the stats, even if the player doesn't meet the requirement to apply it.
Additionally, it grants the Withered Bonus, which grants +1 [[Strength]] per player's [[Catacombs]] level.
|Stat Bonus|Rarity COMMON ([[Rarity]])|Rarity UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|Rarity RARE ([[Rarity]])|Rarity EPIC ([[Rarity]])|Rarity LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|Rarity MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|Rarity DIVINE ([[Rarity]])|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|Stat Bonus|+60 Str ([[Strength]])|+75 Str ([[Strength]])|+90 Str ([[Strength]])|+110 Str ([[Strength]])|+135 Str ([[Strength]])|+170 Str ([[Strength]])|+220 Str ([[Strength]])|
|Reforge Bonus|Withered Bonus; Grants +1 Strength per Catacombs skill level you have.|Withered Bonus; Grants +1 Strength per Catacombs skill level you have.|Withered Bonus; Grants +1 Strength per Catacombs skill level you have.|Withered Bonus; Grants +1 Strength per Catacombs skill level you have.|Withered Bonus; Grants +1 Strength per Catacombs skill level you have.|Withered Bonus; Grants +1 Strength per Catacombs skill level you have.|Withered Bonus; Grants +1 Strength per Catacombs skill level you have.|
|Cost to apply|250,000 [[Coins]]|250,000 [[Coins]]|250,000 [[Coins]]|250,000 [[Coins]]|250,000 [[Coins]]|250,000 [[Coins]]|250,000 [[Coins]]|
## Tips
- While [[Reforging|Fabled]] is the meta for Damage per second, players with lower stats can deal more damage with [[Reforging|Withered]], since the former grants a percentage boost to damage, while the latter grants a lot of stats.
  - When using [[Dark Claymore]] as Left-Click Mage, [[Reforging|Withered]] is always preferred. However when using [[Hyperion]] as Left-Click Mage, [[Reforging|Fabled]] is preferred for its [[Crit Damage]], as Hyperion gives none on its own.
## Trivia
- The [[Reforging|Withered]] reforge gives the most [[Strength]] in the game.

## Usage
Sword:
Wither Cloak Sword [[Defense]]: +250 [[Damage]]: +190 [[Strength]]: +135
[Effects]
[Ability: Creeper Veil RIGHT CLICK; Spawns a layered veil that negates damage for 10s, as follows:; ➤ Lose a layer on hit, consuming 30 Vitality and block up to 1000 damage.; ➤ The damage blocked scales with your Dungeon Stat Boost while in The Catacombs.; ➤ When the damage exceeds a layer's limit, another layer is consumed, until all damage is accounted for.; ➤ When you run out of Vitality, consume all Vitality and take slight knockback.; You cannot attack or regenerate Vitality while the veil is up.; RIGHT CLICK while active to deactivate. Cooldown is halved on deactivation.; Vitality Cost (Vitality): 30; Cooldown: 10s]
The Wither Cloak Sword, being a sword, can be used as a melee weapon. Its Creeper Veil ability can be toggled upon right-click: it has a 10s cooldown between activations, which is halved upon manually toggling the ability off.
The Creeper Veil ability summons 6 invisible Charged Creepers, which last for the duration of the ability. Should the player not have enough [[Vitality]] to absorb another hit, the veil will automatically toggle off.
## Game Messages
|Message|Condition|
|-|-|
|Creeper Veil Activated!|Activating Creeper Veil|
|Creeper Veil De-activated!|Deactivating Creeper Veil manually|
|Creeper Veil De-activated! (Expired)|Creeper Veil deactivating after 10 seconds|
## Essence Crafting
Essence Required; [[Wither Essence]]:
|Action|Cost|Cumul. (Cumulative)|
|-|-|-|
|✪|75 Essence|75 Essence|
|✪✪|150 Essence|225 Essence|
|✪✪✪|300 Essence|525 Essence|
|✪✪✪✪|600 Essence; 10,000 [[Coins]]|1,125 Essence; 10,000 [[Coins]]|
|✪✪✪✪✪|1,200 Essence; 25,000 [[Coins]]|2,325 Essence; 35,000 [[Coins]]|
## Tips
- The Wither Cloak Sword works well when paired with the [[Soulflow Supercell]] or its predecessors, as the Creeper Veil ability forces the player to leave combat, allowing the accessory to increment [[Mana|Overflow Mana]].
- The Wither Cloak Swords ability can be paired with the [[Hurricane Bow]] to kill [[Bal]] much faster.
## Gallery
- A preview of Creeper Veil effect
## Trivia
- The Creeper Veil ability would previously persist even when the item was removed from the player's inventory. This has since been patched.
- The Creeper Veil ability previously dealt 200 damage to enemies that hit the entities spawned from the ability.
- The Wither Cloak Sword can bypass damage from the [[The Watcher|Boomer Undead]]'s TNT and [[Kalhuiki Tribe Member|Kalhuiki Tribe Members]]' fatal attacks.
- The Wither Cloak Sword seems to be the scroll used by [[Necron]] within the journal entry [[Journal|The Apprentice - Part 2]].

# Wizard Wand
Categories: Epic items, Weapons
[Wizard Wand]
Type: Wand ([[Wands (disambiguation)]])
Rarity: EPIC ([[Rarity]])
Obtained: [[Shen's Auction]]
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: WIZARD_WAND
Tooltip: A private joke amongst Wizards, but you wouldn't understand.; Ability: Time Warp! RIGHT CLICK Teleport through time (a work in progress).; This item has no purchasing data yet!; EPIC WAND
Wizard Wand is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Wands|Wand]]. It can be obtained from [[Shen's Auction|Shen's Special]]. It displays the buyer's name, cost paid, and the details about in which auction it was sold and the ranking of the buyer within that auction. This data disappears if the item is traded in any way, and due to a bug, also when it returns from its ability.
## Obtaining
The Wizard Wand can be bid on at [[Shen's Auction|Shen's Special]]. The top 20 bidders receive the item.
## Usage
When used, the Wizard Wand will be consumed, and a message will appear in chat. The Wand will disappear for 3-5 days, before appearing again with +1 [[Intelligence]] (up to a maximum of +100 ).
## Game Message
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|Disappearing|Your Wizard Wand warped in time! But without you... Do not fret! It will be back in 3-5 business days!|
|Reappearing|Your Wizard Wand has re-appeared! Fantastic!|
## Trivia
- Only one Wizard Wand can be lost in time at a time. Attempting to use a second while another is lost will result in the following message:
You already have a Wizard Wand that is lost in time!
## Bugs
- Wizard Wand re-appears without any purchasing data.

## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the XYZ Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Mana Regeneration]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Mana Regeneration ([[Attributes]]); Increases your Mana regeneration by +1%–10%.
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].
|Level|Shards; Required|Total; Shards|Total; SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|Ⅰ (1)|1|1|+1 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅱ (2)|1|2|+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅲ (3)|2|4|+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅳ (4)|2|6|+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅴ (5)|3|9|+5 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅵ (6)|3|12|+6 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅶ (7)|4|16|+7 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅷ (8)|4|20|+8 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅸ (9)|5|25|+9 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅹ (10)|7|32|+10 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
### Fusion
An XYZ Shard can be used in [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]] to create other [[Attributes|Shards]].
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
[Data from SkyShards.]
## Lore
When viewed in the [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] or [[Bazaar]], the XYZ Shard has the following description:
- When Exe encounters its counterparts, it instinctively moves towards them, beginning the process of merging into a more powerful entity.
## Trivia
- Despite being an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Attributes|Shard]], the XYZ Shard does not have a [[Hunting]] level requirement.
## History

|Jun 5, 2025|Added XYZ.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]

|Aug 5, 2025|0.23.3|Renamed XYZ to XYZ Shard.|

# Y
Categories: Epic items, Items in Sacks, Items, Crafting ingredients
[Y]
Type: Item
Rarity: EPIC ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Sacks: Small Nether Sack ([[Nether Sack]]); Medium Nether Sack ([[Nether Sack]]); Large Nether Sack ([[Nether Sack]])
[Shop]
Sell: 30 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: Y
Tooltip: Two Dimensional; Right-click to view recipes!; EPIC
Y is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] item used to craft [[Synthesizer v2]].
## Obtaining
Y can be obtained by using an [[Atominizer]] on a [[Wai]]. A [[Wai]] is spawned when 2 [[Exe|Exes]] come into contact, which can happen after they are lead to each other with [[Charminizer]].
## Usage
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Synthesizer v2]]|None|1x [[Synthesizer v1]] [[Y]] 512x (8 stacks (512 items total)) Y|Recipe: Y x64 ×8 + [[Synthesizer v1]] → [[Synthesizer v2]]|
|Shimmering Light Tunic ([[Shimmering Light Armor]])|Mycelium IX (9) ([[Mycelium]])|5x [[Enchanted Mycelium Cube]] 10x [[Z]] [[Y]] 60x Y|Recipe: [[Enchanted Mycelium Cube]] ×5 + Y x30 ×2 + [[Z]] x10 → [[Shimmering Light Armor]]|
|Shimmering Light Trousers ([[Shimmering Light Armor]])|Mycelium IX (9) ([[Mycelium]])|4x [[Enchanted Mycelium Cube]] 10x [[Z]] [[Y]] 60x Y|Recipe: [[Enchanted Mycelium Cube]] ×4 + [[Z]] x10 + Y x30 ×2 → [[Shimmering Light Armor]]|
### Synthesizers
Up to [[Y]] 30 Y can be placed in [[Synthesizer v2]] to gain +1 [[Defense]] per item, and up to [[Y]] 40 Y can be placed in [[Synthesizer v3]] to gain +1 [[Defense]] per item.
### Essence Crafting
Y can be used to add [[Star Upgrades|Stars]] to [[Shimmering Light Armor]].
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Feb 22, 2022|Added Y.|
|Jun 24, 2026|Added a custom texture for Y as part of the SkyBlock Resource Pack.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Apr 20, 2022|0.13|Added Y.|
|Dec 3, 2024|0.20.9|Y is no longer Co-op Soulbound ([[Soulbound]]).|
|Jul 8, 2026|0.26|Added a custom texture for Y as part of the SkyBlock Resource Pack.|

## Obtaining
### Crafting

|Yog Leggings ([[Yog Armor]])|Hard Stone V (5) ([[Hard Stone]])|1x Flamebreaker Leggings ([[Flamebreaker Armor]]) 40x [[Yoggie]]|Recipe: [[Yoggie]] x5 ×8 + [[Flamebreaker Armor]] → [[Yog Armor]]|

## Usage
Armor Set:
|Helmet|Chestplate|
|-|-|
|Yog Helmet ([[Yog Armor]]); [[Health]]: +50 [[Defense]]: +15 [[Heat Resistance]]: +10 [[Mining Speed]]: +25 [[Mining Fortune]]: +20|Yog Chestplate ([[Yog Armor]]); [[Health]]: +50 [[Defense]]: +15 [[Heat Resistance]]: +10 [[Mining Speed]]: +25 [[Mining Fortune]]: +20|
|Leggings|Boots|
|Yog Leggings ([[Yog Armor]]); [[Health]]: +50 [[Defense]]: +15 [[Heat Resistance]]: +10 [[Mining Speed]]: +25 [[Mining Fortune]]: +20|Yog Boots ([[Yog Armor]]); [[Health]]: +50 [[Defense]]: +15 [[Heat Resistance]]: +10 [[Mining Speed]]: +25 [[Mining Fortune]]: +20|
[Total]
[Yog Armor Health: +200 Defense: +60 Heat Resistance: +40 Mining Speed: +100 Mining Fortune: +80]
[Effects]
[Full Set Bonus: Absorb; Every 5 Yogs killed grants the wearer +1 Mining Speed. (Max 500).]
Yog Armor's Absorb full set bonus grants the player +1 [[Mining Speed]] for every 5 [[Yog|Yogs]] killed in the [[Bestiary]], up to +500 [[Mining Speed]] at 2,500 [[Yog|Yogs]] killed.
## Skins
|Slot|Name|Rarity|Obtaining|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Ablaze Skin]]|[[Ablaze Skin]]|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|[[Event Shop]] - Easter 2025 - 100 Event Silver|
## Trivia
- Yog Armor is referred to as Armor of Yog in the [[Museum]].
- The Absorb full set bonus used to be stored on the Yog Chestplate This meant that if a Yog Chestplate was traded or auctioned, the bonus would transfer over.
- Yog Armor and [[Sorrow Armor]] are the only armor sets that have Universal Gemstone Slots.
- Yog Armor, [[Sorrow Armor]] and [[Armor of Divan]] are the only armor sets that can store [[Gemstone|Topaz]] Gemstones.
- Maxed Yog Armor grants +1420 [[Mining Speed]], which is 60 [[Mining Speed]] more than maxed [[Armor of Divan]].
- Placing a [[Gemstone|Ruby]] in [[Flamebreaker Armor]] and upgrading it into Yog Armor will cause the [[Gemstone|Ruby]] to stop granting [[Health]]. It will be available for refund at the [[Geo|Gemstone Grinder]]: additionally, the following text will display in the tooltip.
This item has unused Gemstones! Visit Geo to remove them!
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Jun 30, 2021|AHN 0.12 Round 1|Added Yog Armor.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Jul 14, 2021|0.12|Added Yog Armor.|
|Sep 17, 2024|0.20.6|Added +25 [[Mining Speed]], +20 [[Mining Fortune]], and +20 [[Heat Resistance]] per piece.|
|Sep 17, 2024|0.20.6|Changed the Absorb bonus from granting bonus [[Mining Speed]] every 10 [[Yog]] kills to every 5 kills.|
|Sep 17, 2024|0.20.6|Removed the Heat Resistance full set bonus. This ability is now on [[Heat Armor]].|

Zombie Armor is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Armor]] Set unlocked at [[Rotten Flesh|Rotten Flesh VIII (8)]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|Zombie Chestplate ([[Zombie Armor]])|Rotten Flesh VIII (8) ([[Rotten Flesh]])|4x [[Enchanted Rotten Flesh]] 4x [[Zombie's Heart]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Rotten Flesh]] ×4 + [[Zombie's Heart]] ×4 → [[Zombie Armor]]|
|Zombie Leggings ([[Zombie Armor]])|Rotten Flesh VIII (8) ([[Rotten Flesh]])|3x [[Zombie's Heart]] 4x [[Enchanted Rotten Flesh]]|Recipe: [[Zombie's Heart]] ×3 + [[Enchanted Rotten Flesh]] ×4 → [[Zombie Armor]]|
|Zombie Boots ([[Zombie Armor]])|Rotten Flesh VIII (8) ([[Rotten Flesh]])|2x [[Enchanted Rotten Flesh]] 2x [[Zombie's Heart]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Rotten Flesh]] ×2 + [[Zombie's Heart]] ×2 → [[Zombie Armor]]|
## Usage
Armor Set:
|Chestplate|Leggings|
|-|-|
|Zombie Chestplate ([[Zombie Armor]]); [[Health]]: +200 [[Defense]]: +40|Zombie Leggings ([[Zombie Armor]]); [[Health]]: +160 [[Defense]]: +30|
|Boots||
|Zombie Boots ([[Zombie Armor]]); [[Health]]: +120 [[Defense]]: +25||
[Total]
[Zombie Armor Health: +480 Defense: +100]
[Effects]
[Full Set Bonus: Projectile Absorption; Heals the wearer for 10 Health per second for 5 seconds when hit by a projectile]
## Essence Crafting
Essence Required; [[Undead Essence]]:
|Action|Chestplate Cost|Chestplate Cumul. (Cumulative)|Leggings Cost|Leggings Cumul.|Boots Cost|Boots Cumul.|Combined Cost|Combined Cumul.|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|Conversion (The cost of the conversion to a dungeon item.)|200|200|150|150|120|120|470|470|
|✪|40|240|40|190|35|155|115|585|
|✪✪|60|300|60|250|50|205|170|755|
|✪✪✪|80|380|80|330|65|270|225|980|
|✪✪✪✪|100; 10,000 [[Coins]]|480; 10,000 [[Coins]]|100; 10,000 [[Coins]]|430; 10,000 [[Coins]]|80; 10,000 [[Coins]]|350; 10,000 [[Coins]]|280; 30,000 [[Coins]]|1,260; 30,000 [[Coins]]|
|✪✪✪✪✪|120; 25,000 [[Coins]]|600; 35,000 [[Coins]]|120; 25,000 [[Coins]]|550; 35,000 [[Coins]]|100; 25,000 [[Coins]]|450; 35,000 [[Coins]]|340; 75,000 [[Coins]]|1,600; 105,000 [[Coins]]|
## Trivia
- As the set doesn't include a [[Helmets|Helmet]], it is one of the few sets with a Full Set Bonus that doesn't require all 4 Armor pieces.
- Oddly enough, it is not considered as an [[Undead Items|Undead]] armor set.
- Donating [[Revenant Armor]] to the [[Museum]] gives credit for this set, despite not having this set in the recipe.

# Zombud
Categories: Epic items, Items in Sacks, Items, Mutations
[Zombud]
Type: Mutation
Rarity: EPIC ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✕ No
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Sacks: Small Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]]); Medium Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]]); Large Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]])
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: ZOMBUD
Tooltip: No one really understands how it works, but somehow this flower can bring the dead back to life, transforming them into... well, zombies.; Analyze this Mutation at the Crop Analyzer in Jake's Underground Lab.; EPIC MUTATION
A Zombud is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Mutations|Mutation]].
## Obtaining
A Zombud can grow in the [[Greenhouse]] if there are 4 [[Dead Plant|Dead Plants]], 2 [[Cindershade|Cindershades]], and 2 [[Fleshtrap|Fleshtraps]] within 1 block of [[Soul Sand]].
While growing, a Zombud does not need water and has 16 Growth Stages. When placed near another crop, it allows for additional items to be found and spreads buffs and debuffs to adjacent crops.
When harvested, a Zombud converts all surrounding [[Dead Plant]] within 1 block into [[Zombuddy]]. Each [[Zombuddy]] slain drops a Zombud.
## Crop Drops
When harvested, Zombuds also drop 765 (11 stacks plus 61) [[Pumpkin]], 1,800 (28 stacks plus 8) [[Wild Rose]], and +7,200 [[Farming|Farming XP]].
## Usage
### Crop Analyzer
A Zombud can be analyzed in the [[Crop Analyzer]] for 1,000,000 [[Coins]]. When analyzed, it grants 500 [[Copper]]. The first time analyzing a Zombud grants an additional 1,250 [[Copper]].
### Mutations
Zombuds are used to create a [[Devourer]].
## Gallery
- Zombud in the Greenhouse Zombud in the [[Greenhouse]]
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Dec 5, 2025|Added Zombud.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Dec 15, 2025|0.24|Added Zombud.|

# Gloves
Aliases: Glove
Categories: Equipment, Gloves
Gloves are pieces of [[Equipment]] that are able to be equipped in the [[Equipment Menu]]. Gloves share the same slot as [[Bracelets]].
